## 2026-10-29 — M6: bounded operator stock and production customer journey transport

- **Criteria:** J1 verified stock service transport; J5 operator quote/order and customer outcome, status and lineage transport; T5 security and T6 regressions.
- **Demonstrated defect:** upstream authenticated `src/app/api/operator/stock/route.ts`, `src/app/api/operator/production/route.ts` and `src/app/api/customer-account/production/route.ts` all buffered `response.json()` without decoded byte limits and read unbounded error response bodies via `response.text()`. A malfunctioning or compromised upstream could allocate unbounded memory even when individual requests carried strict input limits.

Existing shared UTF-8 fail-closed reader reused; eight-second network abort/no-store/owner/operator authorization preserved. Upstream failure text is no longer buffered or echoed.
- **Verification:** new SHA commits confirmed by GitHub writes; tests, quality, engine, preview, release, build and target-width browser checks **PENDING on final integrated head**. Earlier intermediate CI runs may cancel on push; never count cancelled or parent green as this final head's proof.

## 2026-10-31 — M109: private original-create receipt lookup (draft)
- **Parent:** M108 draft #610 exact `b872d7373af31abdeb9ec9d8c2337998a16ebb2e`. Its disposable stock PG #41219374825 and new production-create PG #41219374861 PASS; main CI and independent Non-3D were still in progress on selection, not evidence for M109.
- **Defect:** after a lost POST response or browser crash, M107 preserved a random UUID and blocked writes, and M108's SQL deduplicated exactly that key, but the operator had no way to locate an already committed create when the capped ledger did not contain it. Clearing the hold and recreating with a new key could make duplicates.
- **Change:** additive draft migration `20261107_production_creation_receipt_lookup.sql` service-role-only SQL function returns only (action, receipt UUID) from private request ledger; no writes, personal data or reveal of stored intent hash. Authenticated operator action `creation_receipt_lookup` validates literal original key and typed maximum-two receipt results; fail closed. ### 2026-11-01 — M145: creation-receipt recovery must match BOTH original UUID and write epoch (draft)
- **Criteria:** T5/J5 production recovery integrity; T6/J6 regression evidence. Independent compatible draft child of M144 PR #651, based on exact parent `ddb5234b5341f90de140c6231262772c4a7e10bd`; the parent branch continues receiving fixes and must be reconciled before any integration. No main merge or deployment.
- **Actual defect:** Creation receipt lookup checked only the pending original request UUID after its asynchronous read. A different tab can begin and finish a production write and change the anonymous epoch while a creation receipt lookup is in flight; checking an unchanged UUID alone can present stale recovery evidence as current.
- **Fix:** Capture the exact anonymous hold token **and epoch** before the read using the existing fail-closed write-review snapshot, and require the same validated snapshot after parsing the response before presenting a committed/empty creation receipt. Retain manual review and never clear the hold or retry automatically.
- **Regression:** Extend the creation-receipt source guard and pure snapshot test to keep the original UUID unchanged while the write epoch changes, requiring rejection of the stale evidence. Invalid/corrupt epoch fails closed.
